```
def soft_max_lse(c, beta):
    cmax = c.max()
    shifted = beta*(c-cmax)
    shifted -= shifted.max() maybe for exp
    logsum = ...
    L = cmax + logsum/beta
    weights = exp(shifted - logsum_exp)
    return L, weights
```

So define:
```
def lse_constrained_fg(x, beta, n, n_fft, mu):
   q = np.clip(x,0,None)
   S=q.sum()
   if S <= 1e-12: return (1e100, zeros)
   c=fast_conv_padded(q,n,n_fft)
   cmax= c.max()
   # stable weights
   shifted = beta*(c-cmax)
   maxshift = shifted.max() if finite else 0
   shifted -= maxshift
   exps=np.exp(shifted)
   z=exps.sum()
   L=cmax + (np.log(z)+maxshift)/beta
   w=exps/z
   # penalty
   err=(S - n)/n  # relative
   f = L + mu*err*err
   H = compute_corr_weights_q(q,w,n,n_fft) # length n
   grad = 2.0 * H + 2.0*mu*err/n * np.ones(n)
   return (float(f), grad)
```

Implement efficient projection:
```
def project_simplex(v, target):
  # minimize ||x-v||^2 s.t. x>=0, sum target
  x = np.sort(v)[::-1]
  cums = np.cumsum(x)
  rho = np.nonzero(x > (cums - target)/(np.arange(n)+1))[0][-1]
  tau = (cums[rho] - target)/(rho+1)
  return np.maximum(v - tau, 0)
```
Sort each iteration O(nlogn). For 7000 maybe 0.3ms? 1000=0.3s, okay. Could be 1ms ->1s. Fine.

Let's write `lse_fg(x, beta, n, fft_len, mu):`
- q = np.asarray(x, dtype=float). If scipy passes list? It's ndarray.
- q = np.where(q <0,0,q); if q >100? clip to 100? We can use `np.clip(q,0.0,100.0)`. But modifying x? If x from minimize, we can copy? `q = x.copy(); q[q<0]=0`. Fine.
- S = q.sum(). If S < 1e-9: return (1e100, zeros).
- FQ = np.fft.rfft(q, fft_len)
- c = np.fft.irfft(FQ * FQ, fft_len)[: 2*n - 1]
- cmax = c.max()
- if not finite or cmax <=0: handle.
- shifted = beta * (c - cmax)
  Since shifted ≤0 but may underflow. exp ok. Need if beta large and c-cmax negative large: exp underflows to 0 okay. But if c-cmax has nan? no.
- z = shifted.sum() (exp sum). If z<=0 or not finite: weights uniform? For beta large all exp zero except max possibly 1; if all underflow due max subtract? max shifted=0, exp(0)=1 so z≥1. Good.
- L = cmax + np.log(z)/beta
- weights = np.exp(shifted)/z. To avoid exp twice, use exps.
- err = S/n -1
- f = L + mu*err*err
- FW = np.fft.rfft(weights, fft_len)
- H = np.fft.irfft(FW * np.conj(FQ), fft_len)[:n]
- grad = 2.0 * H + (2.0*mu*err/n) * ones(n)
- return (f, grad)
```
Check gradient of penalty: f_pen=μ(S/n-1)^2 = μ(err)^2; d/dq =2μerr * (1/n) * ones. Good.

If beta==0? Not use.

But does L (softmax) of c approximate max(c) for c large? Yes.

Potential issue: c coefficients can be negative due numerical? q nonnegative, convolution nonnegative, but FFT roundoff may produce tiny negative near zero. We can clip c = np.maximum(c,0). Also weights fine. Clipping changes gradient slightly but okay. cmax might be small.

Now projection onto simplex. Implement for sum target:
```
def project_nonneg_sum(v, target_sum):
    v = np.asarray(v,float)
    x = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    n = x.size
    css = np.cumsum(x)
    rho = np.arange(1,n+1)
    cond = x > (css - target_sum)/rho
    if not cond.any(): return np.full(n, target_sum/n)
    r = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    tau = (css[r-1] - target_sum)/r  # since 0-index
    out = np.maximum(v - tau,0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s > 0: out *= target_sum/s  # correct roundoff
    return out
```
Need if target_sum <=0.

Generate anti-sym:
```
def antisym_random(n, rng, kind):
   m=n//2
   if kind==0: r = 2*rng.integers(0,2,m)-1
   elif kind==1: r = rng.standard_normal(m)
   elif kind==2: # spike? r = cos(π*rng.uniform(...)+...) maybe
   s = np.zeros(n)
   s[:m]=r
   # right: for i in 0..m-1: s[n-1-i] = -r[i]
   s[n-m:] = -r[::-1]  # length m indices n-m..n-1 = -r[m-1..0]; matches s[n-1-i] = -r[i]
   if n%2==1: s[m]=0
   s -= s.mean()
   maxs=np.max(np.abs(s))
   if maxs>0: s/=maxs
   return s
```
For even n=2m, s[n-m:] = s[m:2m] = -r[::-1]. Pair indices: i=m? n-1-(m-1)=m? Good. For n odd n=2m+1, s[:m]=r, s[m]=0, s[m+1:]= -r[::-1]? indices m+1..2m: n-1-i =2m -i. For i=0 ->2m, s[2m]=-r0; slice m+1 length m: first index m+1 corresponds i=m-1? Formula s[n-1-i]= -r[i]. n-1-i=2m-i. i=0 ->2m, i=m-1 ->m+1. So slice reversed = -r[::-1]. Good.

def fourier_q(n, rng, alpha, R=6):
  x=np.arange(n)
  q=np.ones(n)
  for r in range(R):
    f=int(rng.integers(1,max(2,R+1))) # up to 6
    amp = alpha*(0.5/r+0.2)
    q += amp*np.cos(2π f x/n + rng.uniform(0,2π))
    q += amp*np.sin(...)
  q = q - q.mean()
  maxs=np.abs(q).max(); if maxs>0: q=q/maxs
  return 1 + (alpha if alpha<=0.95 else 0.95)*q
```

Now refine_lbfgs top-level details:
```
def refine_lbfgs(q, n, fft_len, beta_schedule, mu, deadline, est_time, cap_fun=30000):
    q = np.asarray(q,float).copy()
    q = normalize_q(q,n)
    best_q = q.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_q(q,n,fft_len)
    if best_sc >= np.inf: return best_sc,best_q
    for beta in beta_schedule:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       # Maybe if beta lower than previous? schedule sorted.
       # Use current q; if it's best and score already near target, still continue.
       rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
       if rem < 8: break
       maxfun = int(min(cap_fun, max(500, rem * 0.85 / max(est_time,1e-5))))
       maxiter = max(100, maxfun // 20)
       bounds = Bounds(0.0, 10.0)
       try:
           res = minimize(lse_fg, q, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B',
                          args=(beta,n,fft_len,mu),
                          bounds=bounds,
                          options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': maxfun,
                                   'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-7,
                                   'maxls': 50, 'maxcor': 30, 'disp': False})
           q2 = np.clip(res.x.astype(float), 0.0, 100.0)
           q2 = normalize_q(q2, n)
           sc2 = exact_score_q(q2, n, fft_len)
           if sc2 < best_sc:
               best_sc = sc2; best_q = q2.copy()
           # Decide next q: if sc2 <= best_sc*1.01 use q2 else maybe keep best? Use q2? Could fallback.
           if sc2 < best_sc * 1.02:
               q = q2
           else:
               # try a small step from best? Use average?
               q = q2  # maybe continue from q2 to allow escape? But if worsened too much, fallback best.
               if sc2 > best_sc * 1.05:
                   q = best_q.copy()
           # If objective no exact improvement, try again? Not in loop.
       except Exception as e:
           # continue
           print('lbfgs error', e)
    # final exact score from best
    return best_sc, best_q
```
Potential: `Bounds` object can be reused; in function create.

def estimate_eval_time(n, fft_len):
   rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
   q = rng.uniform(0.1,2.0,n); q *= n/q.sum()
   lse_fg(q, 10, n, fft_len, 1e4)
   t0=time.monotonic()
   for i in range(3): lse_fg(q,10,n,fft_len,1e4)
   dt=(time.monotonic()-t0)/3
   return max(1e-4, dt)
```

def make_initializations(n, rng, parent, count=16):
   lst=[]
   if parent is not None and parent.size>=64:
      # maybe truncate/pad to n? If length not n, can't. For primary n=parent size. For other lengths, maybe use if same; else skip.

```
def resample_to_length(a, m):
   a=np.clip(a,0,None); n=a.size
   if n==m: return a
   if n>1:
      x=np.arange(n); y=np.arange(m)
      a2=np.interp(y, x, a)
   else: a2=np.full(m,a[0])
   return normalize_q(a2,m)
```

For targeted, top-level `targeted_perturb(q, fft_len, rng, strength)`:
```
def targeted_perturb(q, n, fft_len, rng, strength):
  FQ=rfft(q,fft_len); c=irfft(FQ*FQ,fft_len)[:2n-1]
  top_count= max(3, min(8, 1+n//1000))
  kidx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_count]
  contrib=np.zeros(n)
  for k in kidx:
    js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1)
    contrib[js:je+1] += q[js:je+1][::-1]
  # reduce high contrib, add to low
  q2=q.copy()
  nreduce=max(10, n//50)
  top_indices=np.argsort(contrib)[::-1][:nreduce]
  bottom_indices=np.argsort(contrib)[:-nreduce] if n>2*nreduce else np.argsort(contrib)[:nreduce]
  total=0
  for j in top_indices:
     amt=q2[j]*strength
     q2[j]-=amt; total+=amt
  if len(bottom_indices)>0 and total>0:
     # distribute proportionally to current q? Maybe uniformly
     add=total/len(bottom_indices)
     for j in bottom_indices: q2[j]+=add
  normalize
```
Using Python loops over up to n/50=140, okay. But if top and bottom overlap? bottom exclude top if enough. Good.

Now exact score: values q might have zeros and sum n; c via FFT. Use `np.max(c)`. Since c nonnegative; clip tiny negative.

```
def exact_score_q(q,n,fft_len):
   q=np.clip(q,0,None)
   S=q.sum()
   if S<1e-12: return np.inf
   FQ=np.fft.rfft(q,fft_len)
   c=np.fft.irfft(FQ*FQ,fft_len)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   M=float(c.max())
   if not np.isfinite(M): return np.inf
   return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```
Could use exact_fft smaller? We'll pass common.

def lse_norm_pen_fg(x, beta,n,fft_len,mu):
  q=clip(x,0)
  S=sum
  p=q/S
  FQ=rfft(p,n_fft)
  c=ifft(FQ*FQ)[:L]
  cmax, L, weights
  f = 2*n*L + mu*((S/n)-1)^2
  # gradient wrt p for F: 2n * derivative L
  # derivative L wrt p = 2H_p
  H_p=corr(weights,p,N)[:n]
  g_p=2n*2*H_p =4nH_p
  Gp= np.dot(p,g_p)
  grad = (g_p - Gp)/S + 2*mu*(S/n-1)/n * ones(n)
```

Let's finalize objective original:
```
def lse_fg(x,beta,n,fft_len,mu):
    q = np.clip(x,0.0,100.0)
    S = float(q.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12: return (1e100, np.zeros(n))
    FQ = rfft(q,fft_len)
    c = irfft(FQ*FQ,fft_len)[:2n-1]
    c = maximum(c,0)
    cmax=float(max(c))
    shifted = beta*(c-cmax)
    exps = exp(shifted)
    z = exps.sum()
    if z <=0 or not finite: weights uniform? but exp(0)=1.
    L = cmax + log(z)/beta
    err = S/n -1.0
    f = L + mu*err*err
    w=exps/z
    FW=rfft(w,fft_len)
    H=irfft(FW*conj(FQ),fft_len)[:n]
    grad = 2.0*H + (2.0*mu*err/n)*ones(n)
    return (float(f), grad)
```
Wait gradient of L wrt q: c max is cmax, LSE derivative wrt c = weights. derivative c wrt q = 2H? Yes. So grad L =2H. Good. Penalty relative: f=μ (S/n -1)^2; derivative =2μ/S? Actually f=μ*(S/n-1)^2 = μ/n^2 (S-n)^2; derivative wrt q =2μ/n^2 (S-n)*1 = (2μ/n)(S/n-1)*1. We used 2μ err/n. correct.

Now objective values: L~n, f~n. Gradient H~q mean ~1? For high beta single peak, H_j=q_{k-j}, values O(1), grad O(1). Good.

Now exact score q sum n: score = 2*n*max(c_q)/n^2 =2*max(c_q)/n. If L minimizes max c_q, good.

def fft_mag_q(n,rng):
   N=next_pow2(n)
   z = rng.standard_normal(N) + 1j*rng.standard_normal(N)
   z[0]=0.0
   if N%2==0: z[N//2]=0.0
   q = np.abs(np.fft.ifft(z))**2
   q=q[:n]
   return normalize_q(q,n)
```

`exact_score_q`: Use n_fft common. If q has small negative? clip.
```
def exact_score_q(q,n,fft_len):
   q=np.asarray(q,float)
   q=np.clip(q,0.0,Q_UPPER)
   s=q.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return np.inf
   FQ=np.fft.rfft(q,fft_len)
   c=np.fft.irfft(FQ*FQ,fft_len)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   M=float(np.max(c))
   if not np.isfinite(M): return np.inf
   return float(2.0*n*M/(s*s))
```
For small n? no.

`fast_conv_q` maybe for targeted; use same.

`targeted_perturb`:
```
def targeted_perturb(q,n,rng,strength,fft_len):
   q=np.clip(q,0.0,Q_UPPER).copy()
   FQ=np.fft.rfft(q,fft_len)
   c=np.fft.irfft(FQ*FQ,fft_len)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
   top_count=int(max(1,min(6,1+n//2000)))
   kidx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_count]
   contrib=np.zeros(n)
   for k in kidx:
       js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1)
       contrib[js:je+1] += q[js:je+1][::-1]
   nreduce=max(8, n//100)
   idx=np.argsort(contrib)
   top=idx[-nreduce:]
   bottom=idx[:-nreduce] if n>2*nreduce else idx[:nreduce]
   q2=q.copy()
   total=0.0
   for j in top:
       amt=q2[j]*strength
       q2[j]-=amt
       if q2[j]<0: q2[j]=0
       total += amt
   if bottom.size and total>0:
       # add proportionally to q or random weights? Uniform might add to zeros; okay.
       add=total/bottom.size
       for j in bottom:
           q2[j]+=add
   return normalize_q(q2,n)
```
Here bottom may include top indices if n small; okay. Python loops over 70 for n=7000, okay. `np.argsort(contrib)` returns indices ascending; top last. Good.

`random_perturb`:
```
def random_perturb(q,rng,kind,fft_len):
 n=q.size; x=np.arange(n)/n
 q2=q.copy()
 if kind==0:
   amp=0.03+0.07*rng.random()
   noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
   noise -= noise.mean()
   q2 = q * (1 + amp*noise)
 elif kind==1:
   f1=1+8*rng.random(); ph=2πrng; amp=0.02+0.06*rng.random()
   q2=q*(1+amp*np.cos(2π f1*x+ph))
 elif kind==2:
   # additive smooth
   amp=0.05+0.1*rng.random()
   delta=amp*np.sin(2π (2+5*rng.random())*x+...)
   delta -= delta.mean()
   q2=q+delta*q.mean()
 elif kind==3:
   # spectral phase noise
   FQ=np.fft.rfft(q,fft_len)
   scale=0.01+0.04*rng.random()
   noise = scale*(rng.standard_normal(len(FQ)) + 1j*rng.standard_normal(len(FQ)))
   FQ2=FQ*(1+noise)
   FQ2[0]=abs(FQ2[0])
   if len(FQ2)%2==0: FQ2[len(FQ2)//2]=abs(FQ22[len//2])  # careful variable
   q2=np.fft.irfft(FQ2,fft_len)[:n]
 elif kind==4:
   # binary spikes masked random
   mask=rng.random(n)<0.1
   q2=q*(1 - 0.5*mask) + 0.5*(1-mask)*q.mean()
 else:
   q2=q.copy()
 return normalize_q(q2,n)
```
Need avoid syntax error. For spectral noise, rfft length even? For N even, len(FQ2)=N/2+1, Nyquist index N//2. Set real. If N odd? fft_len power of two >=2, so even. Good.

But FQ2 is complex; `abs` returns float.

def canonical_x(x,n):
   x=np.asarray(x,float)
   x=np.clip(x,0.0,1e300)
   s=x.sum()

   else: x = x * (n/s)  # sum n
   # enforce bounds:
   x = np.clip(x, X_LOW, X_HIGH)
   # rescale to sum n? Clipping can change sum; rescale but may exceed high? If clipped high and rescale, high may exceed. Better iterative? Since shape typical max < high, lower clips tiny; one rescale okay:
   s2=x.sum()
   if s2>0:
      factor = n/s2
      x = x * factor
      # if factor > X_HIGH/x max? unlikely; if any >X_HIGH, clip and renormalize again
      x = np.clip(x, X_LOW, X_HIGH)
      s3=x.sum()
      if s3>0: x = x * (n/s3)
   return np.clip(x, X_LOW, X_HIGH)
```

Let's implement invariant `lse_fg`:
```
def lse_fg(x,beta,n,fft_len):
    x = np.asarray(x,float)
    x = np.clip(x,0.0,1e300)
    S = float(x.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12: return (1e100, zeros)
    scale = n / S
    q = x * scale
    FQ = rfft(q,fft_len)
    c=irfft(FQ*FQ,fft_len)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    shifted=beta*(c-cmax)
    exps=np.exp(shifted)
    z=exps.sum()
    if z<=0 or not finite: exps uniform? but max=0.
    L=cmax+log(z)/beta
    w=exps/z
    FW=rfft(w,fft_len)
    H=irfft(FW*np.conj(FQ),fft_len)[:n]
    gq=2.0*H
    weighted = float(np.dot(x,gq) / S)
    grad = scale * (gq - weighted)
    return (float(L), grad)
```
Check gradient: scale=n/S. If x sum n, scale=1. Good.

Potential issue: If x has zeros and S good, grad for zero coordinates can be negative, line search helps.

def refine_lbfgs(x, n, fft_len, beta_schedule, deadline, cap_fun, est_time):
    x = canonical_x(x,n)
    best_x = x.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_x(x,n,fft_len)
    bounds=[(0.0,OPT_UPPER)]*n
    for beta in beta_schedule:
      rem=deadline-time
      if rem<8 break
      maxfun=int(min(cap_fun, max(500, rem*0.9/est_time)))
      maxiter=max(50, maxfun//15)
      try:
         res=minimize(lse_fg, x, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', args=(beta,n,fft_len), bounds=bounds, options=...)
         x2 = np.clip(res.x,0,OPT_UPPER)
         x2 = canonical_x(x2,n)
         sc2=exact_score_x(...)
         if sc2<best_sc: update
         if sc2 <= best_sc*1.02: x=x2
         else: x=best_x.copy()
      except: pass
    final loop...
    return best_sc,best_x
```

def get_parent_sequence():

    if h is None: return None
    try:
       p = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
       p = np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
       if p.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): return None
       return p
    except Exception:
       return None
```

- `resample_to_length`:
```
def resample_to_length(a,m):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return None
   a=np.clip(a,0,None)
   if a.size==m: q=a
   else:
      xo=np.arange(a.size,dtype=float)
      yi=np.arange(m,dtype=float)
      q=np.interp(yi, xo, a)
   q = to_n_sum(q,m)
   return q
```
`to_n_sum` top-level. If a all zero, to_n_sum returns ones. Good.

- `to_n_sum`:
```
def to_n_sum(x,n):
   x=np.asarray(x,float).copy()
   x=np.clip(x,0.0,1e300)
   s=float(x.sum())
   if s <= 1e-12:
       return np.ones(n)
   x *= n/s
   return x
```

```
def canonical_x(x,n):
   x=np.asarray(x,float).copy()
   x=np.clip(x,0.0,1e300)
   s=float(x.sum())
   if s <= 1e-12:
       x=np.ones(n)
       s=float(n)
   x *= n/s
   if x.size>0:
       mx=float(x.max())
       if mx > OPT_UPPER:
           x *= OPT_UPPER / mx
   # don't rescale after upper clip? If mx<=upper, sum n; if clipped by scaling down, sum< n but invariant. ok.
   x=np.clip(x,0.0,OPT_UPPER)

def exact_score_x(x,n,fft_len):

```
def random_perturb(x,rng,kind,fft_len):
   n=x.size
   q=to_n_sum(x,n)
   xg=np.arange(n)/n
   out=q.copy()
   if kind==0:
      np_=rng.standard_normal(n); np_-=np_.mean(); out=q*(1+0.03*(1+0.5*rng.random())*np_)
   elif kind==1:
      f=1+8*rng.random(); ph=2*np.pi*rng.random(); amp=0.02+0.08*rng.random(); out=q*(1+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*xg+ph))
   elif kind==2:
      f=1+10*rng.random(); amp=0.02+0.1*rng.random(); delta=amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*xg+2*np.pi*rng.random()); delta-=delta.mean(); out=q+delta*q.mean()
   elif kind==3:
      FQ=np.fft.rfft(q,fft_len); scale=0.01+0.05*rng.random(); noise=scale*(rng.standard_normal(FQ.size)+1j*rng.standard_normal(FQ.size)); FQ2=FQ*(1+noise); FQ2[0]=abs(FQ2[0]); if FQ.size>1: FQ2[FQ.size//2]=abs(FQ2[FQ.size//2]); out=np.fft.irfft(FQ2,fft_len)[:n]
   elif kind==4:
      mask=(rng.random(n) < 0.1)
      out=q*(1 - 0.5*mask) + 0.5*mask*q.mean()
   else:
      # targeted? no, kind5 maybe local binary
      idx=rng.choice(n, size=max(1,n//20), replace=False)
      out=q.copy(); out[idx]*= (0.5+0.5*rng.random())
   out=np.clip(out,0,OPT_UPPER)
   return to_n_sum(out,n)
```
`rng.choice` with replace=False for n large and size maybe; if size > n, use all. Could use `rng.permutation(n)[:size]`. But okay. In kind5, `size=max(1,n//20)`; n>20. Good.

Before `np.fft.irfft(FQ2,fft_len)`, FQ2 length is rfft size of fft_len. If noise complex, result real. Set Nyquist. Since fft_len even; FQ2.size=fft_len//2+1; Nyquist index size-1? For N even, rfft length N//2+1, Nyquist is index N//2 = size-1. So set last too: `FQ2[-1]=abs(FQ2[-1])`. We set [0]. Good.

Now `targeted_perturb`:
```
def targeted_perturb(x,n,rng,strength,fft_len):
   q=to_n_sum(x,n)
   FQ=np.fft.rfft(q,fft_len)
   c=np.fft.irfft(FQ*FQ,fft_len)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
   top_count=int(max(1, min(6, 1+n//2000)))
   kidx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_count]
   contrib=np.zeros(n)
   for k in kidx:
      js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1)
      contrib[js:je+1] += q[js:je+1][::-1]
   nred=max(8, n//100)
   order=np.argsort(contrib)
   top=order[-nred:]
   if n > 2*nred: bottom=order[:-nred]
   else: bottom=order[:nred]
   out=q.copy()
   total=0.0
   for j in top:
      amt=out[j]*strength
      out[j]-=amt
      if out[j]<0: out[j]=0
      total+=amt
   if bottom.size>0 and total>0:
      add=total/float(bottom.size)
      for j in bottom:
         out[j]+=add
   return to_n_sum(out,n)
```
If bottom includes top when n small no issue.

Now `arrays_distinct`:
```
def arrays_distinct(q, seqs):
   for s in seqs:
       if s is not None and s.size == q.size:
          diff = q - s
          if float(np.linalg.norm(diff)) < 1e-5:
              return False
   return True
```
Could be slow for many, but few.

def antisym_random(n,rng,kind):
   m=n//2
   s=np.zeros(n,float)
   if m==0: return s
   if kind==0:
      r=2.0*(rng.integers(0,2,m)-1)
   elif kind==1:
      r=rng.standard_normal(m)
   else:
      x=np.arange(m,dtype=float)/(m+1.0)
      freq=1.0+5.0*rng.random()
      r=np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
   s[:m]=r
   s[n-m:]= -r[::-1]
   if n%2==1 and m < n//2? For odd, after s[:m]=r (indices0..m-1), s[n-m:] fills indices m+1.. if n=2m+1, n-m=m+1, correct; middle s[m] remains0. Good. For even, n-m=m, fills m..2m-1, correct.
   s -= s.mean()
   maxs=float(np.max(np.abs(s))) if s.size else 0
   if maxs>0: s /= maxs
   return s
```
For even, does s[n-m:] overlap with s[:m]? n-m=m, second half. Good. For odd, second half length m starts m+1, gap m. Good.

`random_fourier_q`:
```
def random_fourier_q(n,rng,alpha):
   x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   q=np.ones(n)
   R=int(rng.integers(3,8))
   for r in range(R):
      f=int(rng.integers(1, max(2,R+2)))
      a=alpha/(r+1.0)
      q += a*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x/n + 2*np.pi*rng.random())
      q += a*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x/n + 2*np.pi*rng.random())
   q -= q.mean()
   m=float(np.max(np.abs(q)))
   if m>0: q=q/m
   q = 1.0 + alpha*q
   return q
```
Alpha up to 0.9.

def chirp_q(n,rng,idx):
   x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   c=(x - (n-1)/2.0)/max(1.0,(n-1)/2.0)
   freq=0.5+4.0*rng.random()
   amp=0.3+0.3*rng.random()
   phase=2*np.pi*rng.random()
   # choose different chirp based idx? maybe use c^2 + sin
   if idx%2:
      d=amp*np.cos(np.pi*freq*c*c + phase)
   else:
      d=amp*np.sin(np.pi*freq*c*c + phase)
   q=1.0+d; q-=q.mean(); m=abs max; if m>0 q=q/m; q=1.0+0.8*q; return q
```

def fft_mag_q(n,rng):
   N=next_pow2(n)
   z=rng.standard_normal(N)+1j*rng.standard_normal(N)
   z[0]=0.0
   if N%2==0: z[N//2]=0.0
   q=np.abs(np.fft.ifft(z))**2
   q=q[:n]
   return q
```

def refine_lbfgs(x, n, fft_len, beta_schedule, deadline, cap_fun, est_time):
    x = canonical_x(x,n)
    best_x=x.copy()

    for beta in schedule:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 10: break
        maxfun = int(min(cap_fun, max(500, int(rem * 0.9 / max(est_time,1e-6)))))
        maxiter = max(200, maxfun // 10)
        options = {'maxfun': maxfun, 'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-5, 'maxls': 50, 'maxcor': 30, 'disp': False}

If total small, maybe. okay.

Length loop:
```
for li,L in enumerate(lengths):
    if time.monotonic() >= (primary_dead if li==0 else secondary_dead): break
    end = primary_dead if li==0 else secondary_dead
    if end <= time.monotonic() + 20: continue
    fft_len=fft_len_for(L)
    est=get_est(L,fft_len)
    inits=make_initializations(L,rng,parent, 16 if li==0 else 10)
    init_scored=[]
    for qi,q in enumerate(inits):
        sc=exact_score_x(q,L,fft_len)
        if not np.isfinite(sc): sc=1e100
        init_scored.append((sc,qi,q))
    init_scored.sort()
    cand_list=[]
    if parent is not None:
       if parent.size==L: p=parent
       else: p=resample_to_length(parent,L)
       if p is not None: cand_list.append(canonical_x(p,L))
    max_cand = 4 if li==0 else 2
    for sc,qi,q in init_scored:
       x=canonical_x(q,L)
       cand_list.append(x)
       if len(cand_list)>=max_cand: break
    # dedup
    unique=[]
    for x in cand_list:
       if arrays_distinct(x, unique): unique.append(x)
    if not unique: unique=[canonical_x(np.ones(L),L)]
    seg_end=end
    seg_start=time.monotonic()
    rem_segment = max(0.0, seg_end - seg_start)
    piece = rem_segment / max(1,len(unique))
    beta_primary = [20.0,50.0,100.0,200.0,400.0,800.0,1600.0,3200.0]
    if li>0: beta_secondary = [50.0,200.0,800.0,3200.0]
    else: beta_use=beta_primary
    cap = 40000 if L <= 12000 else 20000
    for ci,x in enumerate(unique):
       deadline = seg_start + (ci+1)*piece
       if deadline > seg_end: deadline=seg_end
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 10: break
       sc,xout=refine_lbfgs(x,L,fft_len,beta_use, deadline, cap, est)
       if sc < best_score: update; print
```
Issue: `seg_start` actual at start of length. If previous length used less than its end, seg_start still early, so rem_segment large; good. But if time already past seg_end (due previous overrun? We don't overrun planned? refine stops at deadline, so time <= end. It could finish early, so time < seg_end. Good.) `piece` based on remaining from actual to seg_end. If only one cand, it gets all. Good.

But if a candidate takes full piece, next deadline based on original seg_start + (ci+1)*piece, not actual. If previous took less, next gets more; if previous took exactly, okay. It ensures total not beyond seg_end. Good.

Candidate max_cand=4; if all top inits distinct. Parent might be duplicate with inits? We distinguish.

Each refine with beta schedule and final polish may use until deadline? Wait refine is given deadline = piece end. If it doesn't finish beta schedule early, it will continue final polish loop until deadline. So first candidate uses its full piece. Good.

      if rem < 40: break

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2
by optimizing a scale-invariant log-sum-exp approximation of the convolution
peak, followed by targeted restarts and local peak reductions.

The optimizer works on an infinite-scale equivalence class:
    x -> x / sum(x)
so the L-BFGS objective is invariant under global scaling.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

OPT_UPPER = 1000.0
EST_CACHE = {}


def get_parent_sequence():
    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if h is None:
        return None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = p[np.isfinite(p)]
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size == 0:
            return None
        return p
    except Exception:
        return None


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fft_len_for(n):
    return next_pow2(3 * int(n) - 2)


def to_n_sum(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1e300)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    x *= float(n) / s
    return x


def canonical_x(x, n):
    """Keep optimizer variable in a numerically nice scale and bounds."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1e300)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    x *= float(n) / s
    mx = float(x.max())
    if mx > OPT_UPPER:
        x *= OPT_UPPER / mx
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, OPT_UPPER)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return x


def exact_score_x(x, n, fft_len):
    """Exact score for scale-invariant x."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1e300)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    q = x * (float(n) / s)
    fq = np.fft.rfft(q, fft_len)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fq * fq, fft_len)[: 2 * int(n) - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    qsum = float(q.sum())
    return float(2.0 * int(n) * m / (qsum * qsum))


def lse_fg(x, beta, n, fft_len):
    """
    Scale-invariant log-sum-exp objective plus gradient.
    Returns (f, grad) for scipy.optimize.minimize with jac=True.
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1e300)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)

    scale = float(n) / s
    q = x * scale

    fq = np.fft.rfft(q, fft_len)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fq * fq, fft_len)[: 2 * int(n) - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)

    shifted = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
    # Numerical guard: shifted should be <= 0.
    shifted = np.minimum(shifted, 0.0)
    exps = np.exp(shifted)
    z = float(exps.sum())
    if z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(z):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)

    lse_val = cmax + float(np.log(z)) / float(beta)
    w = exps / z

    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, fft_len)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fq), fft_len)[: int(n)]
    h = np.real(h)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)

    gq = 2.0 * h
    weighted = float(np.dot(x, gq) / s)
    grad = scale * (gq - weighted)
    return float(lse_val), grad


def get_est(n, fft_len):
    key = int(n)
    if key in EST_CACHE:
        return EST_CACHE[key]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    x = to_n_sum(rng.uniform(0.1, 2.0, int(n)), int(n))
    lse_fg(x, 20.0, int(n), int(fft_len))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    cnt = 5
    for _ in range(cnt):
        lse_fg(x, 20.0, int(n), int(fft_len))
    dt = (time.monotonic() - t0) / float(cnt)
    dt = max(1e-5, dt)
    EST_CACHE[key] = dt
    return dt


def resample_to_length(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.size == int(m):
        return to_n_sum(a, int(m))
    xo = np.arange(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    yi = np.arange(int(m), dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(yi, xo, a)
    return to_n_sum(b, int(m))


def antisym_random(n, rng, kind):
    n = int(n)
    s = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = n // 2
    if m == 0:
        return s
    if kind == 0:
        r = 2.0 * (rng.integers(0, 2, m) - 1)
        r = r.astype(np.float64)
    elif kind == 1:
        r = rng.standard_normal(m)
    else:
        x = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, m))
        freq = 1.0 + 5.0 * rng.random()
        r = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    s[:m] = r
    s[n - m:] = -r[::-1]
    s -= float(s.mean())
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(s)))
    if mx > 0.0:
        s /= mx
    return s


def random_fourier_q(n, rng, alpha):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    r = int(rng.integers(3, 8))
    for i in range(r):
        f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, r + 2)))
        a = float(alpha) / float(i + 1)
        q += a * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x / float(n) + 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random())
        q += a * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x / float(n) + 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random())
    q -= q.mean()
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(q)))
    if mx > 0.0:
        q = q / mx
    return 1.0 + min(0.95, alpha) * q


def chirp_q(n, rng, idx):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = (x - (n - 1) / 2.0) / max(1.0, (n - 1) / 2.0)
    freq = 0.5 + 4.0 * rng.random()
    amp = 0.3 + 0.3 * rng.random()
    phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
    if idx % 2 == 0:
        d = amp * np.cos(np.pi * freq * c * c + phase)
    else:
        d = amp * np.sin(np.pi * freq * c * c + phase)
    q = 1.0 + d
    q -= q.mean()
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(q)))
    if mx > 0.0:
        q = q / mx
    return 1.0 + 0.8 * q


def sine_q(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    f1 = 1.0 + 5.0 * rng.random()
    f2 = 2.0 + 8.0 * rng.random()
    a1 = 0.2 + 0.4 * rng.random()
    a2 = 0.1 + 0.3 * rng.random()
    q = (
        1.0
        + a1 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * x + 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random())
        + a2 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * x + 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random())
    )
    q -= q.mean()
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(q)))
    if mx > 0.0:
        q = q / mx
    return 1.0 + 0.8 * q


def fft_mag_q(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    N = next_pow2(n)
    z = rng.standard_normal(N) + 1j * rng.standard_normal(N)
    z[0] = 0.0
    if N % 2 == 0:
        z[N // 2] = 0.0
    q = np.abs(np.fft.ifft(z)) ** 2
    return q[:n]


def make_initializations(n, rng, parent, count):
    n = int(n)
    out = []

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        if parent.size == n:
            p = parent
        else:
            p = resample_to_length(parent, n)
        if p is not None:
            out.append(canonical_x(p, n))

    out.append(canonical_x(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n))

    for i in range(6):
        kind = i % 3
        alpha = min(0.95, 0.35 + 0.12 * i)
        s = antisym_random(n, rng, kind)
        q = 1.0 + alpha * s
        out.append(canonical_x(q, n))

    for i in range(3):
        alpha = 0.35 + 0.18 * i
        out.append(canonical_x(random_fourier_q(n, rng, alpha), n))

    for i in range(3):
        out.append(canonical_x(chirp_q(n, rng, i), n))

    for _ in range(2):
        out.append(canonical_x(sine_q(n, rng), n))

    for _ in range(2):
        out.append(canonical_x(fft_mag_q(n, rng), n))

    # Trim if requested, but keep at least a few.
    want = max(4, int(count))
    if len(out) > want:
        out = out[:want]
    return out


def arrays_distinct(q, seqs):
    for s in seqs:
        if s is not None and s.size == q.size:
            diff = q - s
            if float(np.linalg.norm(diff)) < 1e-5:
                return False
    return True


def refine_lbfgs(x, n, fft_len, beta_schedule, deadline, cap_fun, est_time):
    n = int(n)
    fft_len = int(fft_len)
    x = canonical_x(x, n)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_x(x, n, fft_len)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_x = canonical_x(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)
        best_sc = exact_score_x(best_x, n, fft_len)
        if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
            best_sc = 1e100

    bounds = [(0.0, OPT_UPPER)] * n
    schedule = list(beta_schedule)
    if len(schedule) == 0:
        schedule = [1000.0, 3000.0, 8000.0]

    for beta in schedule:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 12.0:
            break
        maxfun = int(min(float(cap_fun), max(800.0, rem * 0.9 / max(float(est_time), 1e-6))))
        maxiter = max(300, maxfun // 10)
        opts = {
            "maxfun": maxfun,
            "maxiter": maxiter,
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-5,
            "maxls": 50,
            "maxcor": 30,
            "disp": False,
        }
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lse_fg,
                x,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                args=(float(beta), n, fft_len),
                bounds=bounds,
                options=opts,
            )
            x2 = np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, OPT_UPPER)
            x2 = canonical_x(x2, n)
            sc2 = exact_score_x(x2, n, fft_len)
            if sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc2
                best_x = x2.copy()
            if sc2 <= best_sc * 1.02:
                x = x2
            else:
                x = best_x.copy()
        except Exception:
            continue

    final_beta = float(schedule[-1])
    stagnant = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 12.0:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 12.0:
            break
        maxfun = int(min(float(cap_fun), max(1500.0, rem * 0.9 / max(float(est_time), 1e-6))))
        maxiter = max(300, maxfun // 10)
        opts = {
            "maxfun": maxfun,
            "maxiter": maxiter,
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-5,
            "maxls": 50,
            "maxcor": 30,
            "disp": False,
        }
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lse_fg,
                x,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                args=(final_beta, n, fft_len),
                bounds=bounds,
                options=opts,
            )
            x2 = np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, OPT_UPPER)
            x2 = canonical_x(x2, n)
            sc2 = exact_score_x(x2, n, fft_len)
            if sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc2
                best_x = x2.copy()
                x = x2
                stagnant = 0
            else:
                if sc2 < best_sc * 1.01:
                    x = x2
                else:
                    x = best_x.copy()
                stagnant += 1
                if stagnant >= 3:
                    break
        except Exception:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant >= 3:
                break

    return best_sc, best_x


def targeted_perturb(x, n, rng, strength, fft_len):
    n = int(n)
    q = to_n_sum(x, n)
    fq = np.fft.rfft(q, fft_len)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fq * fq, fft_len)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    top_count = int(max(1, min(6, 1 + n // 2000)))
    kidx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_count]
    contrib = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in kidx:
        js = max(0, int(k) - n + 1)
        je = min(int(k), n - 1)
        contrib[js : je + 1] += q[js : je + 1][::-1]

    nred = max(8, n // 100)
    order = np.argsort(contrib)
    top = order[-nred:]
    if n > 2 * nred:
        bottom = order[:-nred]
    else:
        bottom = order[:nred]

    out = q.copy()
    total = 0.0
    for j in top:
        amt = out[j] * float(strength)
        out[j] -= amt
        if out[j] < 0.0:
            out[j] = 0.0
        total += float(amt)

    if bottom.size > 0 and total > 0.0:
        add = total / float(bottom.size)
        for j in bottom:
            out[j] += add

    return to_n_sum(out, n)


def random_perturb(x, rng, kind, fft_len):
    n = int(x.size)
    q = to_n_sum(x, n)
    xv = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    out = q.copy()

    if kind == 0:
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
        noise -= noise.mean()
        amp = 0.03 * (1.0 + 0.5 * rng.random())
        out = q * (1.0 + amp * noise)
    elif kind == 1:
        f = 1.0 + 8.0 * rng.random()
        ph = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
        amp = 0.02 + 0.08 * rng.random()
        out = q * (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * xv + ph))
    elif kind == 2:
        f = 1.0 + 10.0 * rng.random()
        amp = 0.02 + 0.10 * rng.random()
        phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
        delta = amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * xv + phase)
        delta -= delta.mean()
        out = q + delta * float(q.mean())
    elif kind == 3:
        fq = np.fft.rfft(q, fft_len)
        scale = 0.01 + 0.05 * rng.random()
        noise = scale * (
            rng.standard_normal(fq.size) + 1j * rng.standard_normal(fq.size)
        )
        fq2 = fq * (1.0 + noise)
        fq2[0] = abs(fq2[0])
        fq2[-1] = abs(fq2[-1])
        out = np.fft.irfft(fq2, fft_len)[:n]
    elif kind == 4:
        mask = rng.random(n) < 0.1
        out = q * (1.0 - 0.5 * mask.astype(np.float64)) + 0.5 * mask.astype(np.float64) * float(q.mean())
    else:
        size = max(1, n // 20)
        if size >= n:
            idx = np.arange(n)
        else:
            idx = rng.choice(n, size=size, replace=False)
        out = q.copy()
        for j in idx:
            out[j] *= 0.5 + 0.5 * rng.random()

    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, OPT_UPPER)
    return to_n_sum(out, n)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_deadline = t_start + max(30.0, float(budget_s)) - 10.0
    eff = max(60.0, total_deadline - t_start)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent = get_parent_sequence()

    # Choose primary and secondary lengths. Stay within a 32768-fft range where
    # possible: 3n-2 <= 32768  =>  n <= 10922.
    if parent is not None and 2048 <= parent.size <= 10922:
        primary_n = int(parent.size)
    elif parent is not None and parent.size > 10922:
        primary_n = 10922
    else:
        primary_n = 8192

    lengths = [primary_n]
    if primary_n < 10922:
        lengths.append(10922)
    lengths = sorted(set(lengths))
    # Keep primary first even if sorting changes order.
    if lengths and lengths[0] != primary_n:
        lengths.remove(primary_n)
        lengths.insert(0, primary_n)

    if len(lengths) > 1:
        primary_dead = t_start + 0.50 * eff
        secondary_dead = t_start + 0.72 * eff
    else:
        primary_dead = t_start + 0.68 * eff
        secondary_dead = primary_dead

    post_deadline = total_deadline - 180.0
    if post_deadline < secondary_dead:
        post_deadline = secondary_dead + 60.0
    local_deadline = total_deadline - 10.0
    if local_deadline < post_deadline + 30.0:
        local_deadline = post_deadline + 30.0

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_seq = None

    print(f"This-SOTA start time: {t_start:.2f}, deadline={total_deadline:.2f}")
    print(f"Lengths: {lengths}")

    for li, n in enumerate(lengths):
        n = int(n)
        seg_end = primary_dead if li == 0 else secondary_dead
        seg_start = time.monotonic()
        if seg_end <= seg_start + 20.0:
            continue

        fft_len = fft_len_for(n)
        est = get_est(n, fft_len)
        print(f"\nLength {n}, fft_len={fft_len}, est_eval_time={est:.6f}s, seg_end={seg_end:.1f}")

        if li == 0:
            inits = make_initializations(n, rng, parent, 18)
        else:
            inits = make_initializations(n, rng, parent, 10)

        init_scored = []
        for qi, q in enumerate(inits):
            sc = exact_score_x(q, n, fft_len)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                sc = 1e100
            init_scored.append((sc, qi, q))
        init_scored.sort()

        cand_list = []
        if parent is not None:
            if parent.size == n:
                p = parent
            else:
                p = resample_to_length(parent, n)
            if p is not None:
                cand_list.append(canonical_x(p, n))

        max_cand = 4 if li == 0 else 2
        for sc, qi, q in init_scored:
            cand_list.append(canonical_x(q, n))
            if len(cand_list) >= max_cand:
                break

        unique_cand = []
        for x in cand_list:
            if arrays_distinct(x, unique_cand):
                unique_cand.append(x)

        if len(unique_cand) == 0:
            unique_cand.append(canonical_x(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n))

        rem_segment = max(0.0, seg_end - time.monotonic())
        if rem_segment < 25.0:
            init_best = min(exact_score_x(x, n, fft_len) for x in unique_cand)
            best_c = unique_cand[0]
            for x in unique_cand[1:]:
                if exact_score_x(x, n, fft_len) < init_best:
                    init_best = exact_score_x(x, n, fft_len)
                    best_c = x
            if init_best < best_score:
                best_score = init_best
                best_seq = best_c.copy()
            continue

        piece = rem_segment / float(max(1, len(unique_cand)))

        if li == 0:
            beta_use = [20.0, 50.0, 100.0, 200.0, 400.0, 800.0, 1600.0, 3200.0]
            cap = 40000 if n <= 12000 else 20000
        else:
            beta_use = [50.0, 200.0, 800.0, 3000.0]
            cap = 30000 if n <= 12000 else 15000

        for ci, x0 in enumerate(unique_cand):
            deadline = seg_start + (ci + 1) * piece
            if deadline > seg_end:
                deadline = seg_end
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 15.0:
                break

            sc, xout = refine_lbfgs(x0, n, fft_len, beta_use, deadline, cap, est)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = xout.copy()
                print(f"  [len={n}, init#{ci}] new best: {sc:.8f}")

    # Fallback if everything failed.
    if best_seq is None:
        n = primary_n
        best_seq = canonical_x(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)
        best_score = exact_score_x(best_seq, n, fft_len_for(n))
        print("Fallback to uniform.")

    # Post-polish global best.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < post_deadline - 20.0:
        n_best = int(best_seq.size)
        fft_best = fft_len_for(n_best)
        est_best = get_est(n_best, fft_best)
        beta_post = [800.0, 2000.0, 5000.0, 10000.0]
        sc, xout = refine_lbfgs(
            best_seq, n_best, fft_best, beta_post,
            post_deadline, 50000, est_best
        )
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = xout.copy()
            print(f"  [post-polish] new best: {sc:.8f}")

    # Local restarts / targeted perturbations on best known sequence.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < local_deadline - 30.0:
        n_best = int(best_seq.size)
        fft_best = fft_len_for(n_best)
        est_best = get_est(n_best, fft_best)
        beta_local = [800.0, 2000.0, 5000.0, 9000.0]
        trial = 0
        while time.monotonic() < local_deadline - 25.0:
            rem = local_deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 45.0:
                break
            piece = min(70.0, max(25.0, rem * 0.25))
            deadline = time.monotonic() + piece
            kind = trial % 5
            if kind == 0:
                strength = 0.02 + 0.07 * rng.random()
                pert = targeted_perturb(best_seq, n_best, rng, strength, fft_best)
            else:
                pert = random_perturb(best_seq, rng, kind, fft_best)

            sc, xout = refine_lbfgs(
                pert, n_best, fft_best, beta_local, deadline, 12000, est_best
            )
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = xout.copy()
                print(f"  [local#{trial}, kind={kind}] new best: {sc:.8f}")
            else:
                print(f"  [local#{trial}, kind={kind}] no improve: {sc:.8f}")
            trial += 1

    # Final safe output.
    out_arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out_arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 64.0
    out_arr = out_arr / s

    # Optional verification with the provided evaluator if available.
    try:
        if time.monotonic() < total_deadline - 6.0 and out_arr.size <= 12000:
            lst = [float(v) for v in out_arr]
            v = evaluate_sequence(lst)
            if np.isfinite(v):
                print("Verified evaluator score:", v)
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"FINAL internal score: {best_score:.10f}, length={out_arr.size}")
    return [float(v) for v in out_arr]
```
